Algorithm for fixed n:
- Generate starts (ones, two-block, harmonic, previous upscaled).
- For each start, normalize target = sqrt(2n). Evaluate score via FFT.
- Run lp_iteration(a, budget). It iterates:
  c=conv(a); M=max(c); S=sum(a)=target.
  Solve LP maximize ∑g s.t. lower_toeplitz(a) g ≤ M.
  If fail or Sopt <= target*(1+1e-6): break.
  g_norm = g*Sopt? Actually g sum Sopt; normalize to target: g_target = g * target/Sopt.
  For t list: a_cand = (1-t)*a + t*g_target; normalize target; score=fast; accept if score <= current*(1+eps), else reduce t. Maybe also accept small improvements. Keep best_score/best.
  Update a to accepted (candidate with best among t). If no accept, maybe use g_target if score better? else break.
  Stop if Sopt/target < 1+0.002 or max iterations or time.
- Return best.

LP local details:
```
def lp_local(a, budget_s, seed=0):
    n=len(a); target=...
    a=normalize(a,target); best=a; best_score=score_fast
    deadline=t0+budget
    it=0
    while time<deadline-5:
        c=conv; M=max
        # If M approx best, maybe skip? anyway
        try:
           C = build_convol(A)
           res=linprog(-ones, A_ub=C, b_ub=ones, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit':5.0, 'output_flag':0})
           if res.status!=0: break
           g=res.x; V=sum(g)
           if V <= target*(1+1e-6): break
           g_target = target/V*g
           g_score=score_fast(g_target)
           candidates_updates: g_target if better
           # line search t [0.5,1.0,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.8]
           accepted=False
           for t in (0.5,0.8,1.0,0.25,0.1,0.05):
               cand=(1-t)*a + t*g_target; norm; sc=score_fast
               if sc < best_score*(1-1e-9): update best; if sc < current: accept a=cand; score=sc; accepted=True; break
           if not accepted:
               # maybe if best improved by g but not current? set a=best?
               break? Or continue with a=best
        except Exception: break
        it+=1
    return best,best_score
```

def smooth_local(a, budget_s, seed=0, beta_start=6.0, beta_end=250.0, eta0=0.25):
    a=normalize(a,target)
    if not finite or budget<1: return a,score
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    deadline ...
    it=0; eta=eta0; beta=beta_start; no_improve=0
    best_score; best=a.copy(); current=a.copy(); score=fast
    # maybe set small floor
    floor = max(1e-12, target*1e-12)
    while time<deadline-0.5:
       it+=1
       # ensure a positive? a = np.maximum(a, 0); if sum small reset
       # c already have from previous? Initially compute; after accept update c.
       maxc=np.max(c); score=float(2*n*maxc/s*s) # s target
       if score < best_score*(1-1e-12): best...
       if no_improve>... restart
       # compute beta retrograde?
       frac=it/2000; beta=beta_start*(beta_end/beta_start)**frac? exponential.
       weights = softmax(beta*(c-maxc))
       grad=2*crosscorr(a, weights)
       lam=(a*grad).sum()/target
       d=grad-lam
       scale=np.max(np.abs(d))
       if scale<1e-14: no_improve+=1; continue
       # smooth noise: n_low random low freq
       if it%? add noise by modifying d? Better add to step.
       step = -eta*d/scale
       if noise>1e-4:
           nz = smooth_random(n, rng, noise)
           step += nz
       step=clip(step,-3,3)
       cand = a*np.exp(step); cand=normalize(cand,target)
       cand_c=conv(cand); cand_score=score
       if cand_score <= score + 1e-10: accept; score=cand_score; no_improve=0; eta=min(1.0,eta*1.05)
       else:
           eta=max(0.02, eta*0.8); no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 800:
           a=smooth_perturb(best,rng,0.3); c=conv; score=...
           beta=beta_start; eta=0.25; no_improve=0
    return best,best_score

def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    a=a.copy(); n=len(a)
    if n>2:
       t=linspace
       z=zeros
       for m in 1..5:
           z += rng.normal(0, strength/(m+1))*cos(2π m t + phase)
       a *= exp(z)
    # random local block
    if n>8:
       width=...; start=...
       a[start:stop] *= exp(rng.normal(0, strength, width))
    return normalize(a,target)

def make_binomial(n):
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1
    for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*(i-1)+1)/(2*i)  # c_i = c_{i-1}*(2i-1)/(2i)? For i=1:1*1/2=0.5; i=2:0.5*3/4=0.375.
    return normalize
```

```
def make_harmonic(n,rng, max_freq=6):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    z=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1,max_freq+1):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.0,1.5)/(m**0.8)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       z += amp*np.cos(2π*m*t+ph)
    # optional linear trend
    z += rng.uniform(-1,1)*(2t-1)
    z -= np.max(z)
    a=np.exp(z)
    return normalize
```
This can produce peaks at edges/center.

Sanitize final:
```
def sanitize(seq):
   arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   if arr.size==0: return [1.0]
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<0.01:
       # scale up taking care max<=1000
       if s>0: arr*=0.01/s
       else: arr=np.ones(arr.size) if size>0 else [1]
   mx=arr.max()
   if mx>1000 and mx>0: arr*=1000/mx
   # do not force mean
   return [float(x) for x in arr]
```

`normalize` implementation:
```
def normalize_sequence(a, target, cap=1000.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    if a.size==0: return empty? We'll handle.
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,cap)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
       a.fill(target/a.size); return a
    for _ in range(5):
       a *= target/s
       a=np.clip(a,0,cap)
       s2=a.sum()
       if abs(s2-target)<1e-12*target: return a
       s=s2
    if s>0: a*=target/s
    return a
```
If cap binding, sum may be lower than target. For our sequences max low; no issue. If cap binding, objective clamp. Could reduce target? Not.

    elif budget<600: levels=[512,1024]
    elif budget<1200: levels=[512,1024,2048]
    else: levels=[512,1024,2048,4096]
    # allocate weights maybe [1,1.5,2,2.5]

def run_level(n, budget, parent, seed, pool):
    if budget<20: return upsample parent
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    deadline=t0+budget
    starts=generate_starts(n,rng,parent, random_count=min(70, 20+n//50))
    # quick smooth on top k
    k_quick = max(1, min(4, int(budget/60)+1, len(starts)))
    quick_budget = 0.45*budget if n<=1024 else 0.5*budget
    time_each=quick_budget/k_quick
    best=None; best_score=inf
    for sc, seq in starts[:k_quick]:
       if time>deadline-10: break
       t_this=min(time_each, deadline-time-5)
       seq2, sc2 = smooth_local(seq, t_this, seed+i, beta_start=8,beta_end=200)
       if sc2<best_score: best=seq2; best_score=sc2
       add to pool
    # ensure at least best from starts
    if best is None: best=starts[0][1]; best_score=starts[0][0]
    # LP
    elapsed=time; remain=deadline-time
    if n<=2048 and remain>80 and HAS_SCIPY:
       lp_budget=min(0.5*remain, 400 if n<=1024 else 250)
       seq2,sc2=lp_local(best, lp_budget, seed)
       update
    # final smooth on best (and maybe one other top start)
    remain=deadline-time
    if remain>20:
       seq2,sc2=smooth_local(best, min(remain*0.8, 300?), seed+999, beta_start=20,beta_end=500)
       update
       remain=...
       # second basin: pick a start from starts not used (e.g. random) if remain>60
       if remain>60:
           other = starts[k_quick] if len>... else generate random
           seq2,sc2=smooth_local(other, remain*0.8, seed+1234)
           update
    return best,best_score
```

Let's design `run_level` more deterministic:
```
def run_level(n, budget_s, parent, seed=0):
    start_time=time
    deadline=start_time+budget_s
    if budget_s<25: return upsample parent
    starts=generate...
    # choose top 3
    k=min(3, len(starts))
    quick_total=0.4*budget_s
    best_seq=None; best_score=inf
    for i in range(k):
        t=time
        if t>deadline-15: break
        each=(quick_total - (t-start_time - elapsed_start?))/ (k-i)
        seq,sc=smooth_local(starts[i][1], max(5, each), seed=seed*10+i)
        update best; add pool
    # LP
    rem=deadline-time
    if n<=2048 and rem>60 and HAS_SCIPY:
        lp_budget=min(rem*0.45, 300 if n<=1024 else 200)
        seq,sc=lp_local(best_seq, lp_budget, seed=seed+100)
        update
    # polish
    rem=deadline-time
    if rem>20:
        seq,sc=smooth_local(best_seq, rem*0.85, seed=seed+200, beta_start=30, beta_end=600, eta0=0.15)
        update
    # optional second basin
    rem=deadline-time
    if rem>30:
        # pick another top start not already refined, e.g. starts[min(k, len-1)][1]
        seq,sc=smooth_local(starts[min(k, len(starts)-1)][1], rem*0.8, seed=seed+300)
        update
    return best
```

    C=build...

    return res

Let's detail helper functions in code.

1. Imports: `import numpy as np, time, math`. SciPy optional.

Helper `make_fft_size(L)`: return 1 << (L-1).bit_length() if L>1 else 1.

`fast_convolution(a, n_overrides=None)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
n=a.size; L=2*n-1
if n==0: return empty
N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
c=np.ascontiguousarray(c)
np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
# Due to roundoff, c may have tiny negative; set to 0.
return c
```
If a has very small negative? normalize clips.

`fast_score_array(a)`:
```
s=a.sum(); if s<0.01: return inf
c=fast_convolution(a); maxc=c.max(); return 2*n*maxc/(s*s)
```

`cross_corr_weights(a, w)`:
```
n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=...
za=np.fft.rfft(a,N); zw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
g=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(za)*zw,N)[:n]
# crosscorr might have tiny negatives; don't clip because gradient can be negative? g is sum positive a*weights positive, should nonnegative. Clip 0.
np.maximum(g,0,out=g)
return 2.0*g
```
Weights nonnegative. If due roundoff negative tiny, clip.

`softmax_weights(c, beta, K=None)`:
```
m=np.max(c)
if not finite: zeros
if beta<=0: return ones/L
z=beta*(c-m)
# clip lower -700
z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
w=np.exp(z)
s=w.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite:
    if K is None: K=max(1,min(len(c), int(0.02*len(c))+1))
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    w=zeros; w[idx]=1; s=K
else: w/=s
return w
```
`argpartition` for K=L returns? handle K>=L: return uniform.

We should track best exact score (fast). If no improvement in best for many, but current may wander. We use no_improve on best, not current. If no best improvement, restart from best with perturbation.

Pseudo smooth_local code (no nested functions):
```
def smooth_local(a, budget_s, seed=0, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=250.0, eta0=0.22):
    n=int(len(a)); target=math.sqrt(max(1.0,2.0*n))
    a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return a, inf
    floor=max(1e-15, target*1e-12)
    a=np.maximum(a,floor); a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
    c=fast_convolution(a); score=score_from_c(a,c)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0.0,budget_s)
    eta=eta0; beta=beta_start; it=0; no_best=0
    # initial symtry maybe
    sym_a, sym_s = try_sym_variants(a)
    if sym_s<score: ...
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
       it+=1
       if not finite: reset to best
       # compute beta based on elapsed/budget if budget>1
       if budget_s>1:
          prog=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/budget_s)
          beta=beta_start*(beta_end/beta_start)**prog
       # soft weights
       maxc=float(np.max(c))
       # If maxc==0? break
       weights=softmax_weights(c,beta)
       grad=gradient_weights(a,weights)
       lam=float(np.dot(a,grad))/target
       d=grad-lam
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if scale<1e-14:
           no_best+=1
           if no_best>60:
              a=smooth_perturb(best,rng,0.25); c=...
              beta=beta_start*0.5; eta=max(eta,0.2)
              no_best=0
           continue
       step=(-eta/scale)*d
       noise_amp = 0.02*np.exp(-0.5*prog)  # gradually small
       if noise_amp>1e-5:
           step += noise_amp*smooth_lowfreq(n,rng,0.25)
       step=np.clip(step,-2.5,2.5)
       cand=a*np.exp(step)
       cand=np.maximum(cand,floor)
       cand=normalize_sequence(cand,target)
       c2=fast_convolution(cand)
       score2=score_from_c(cand,c2)
       if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 <= score + 1e-10:
           # accept if not much worse; maybe also require <=best_score*(1+0.02)
           a=cand; c=c2; score=score2
           if score2 < best_score*(1-1e-12):
               best=a.copy(); best_score=score2; no_best=0
           else:
               # accepted current but no best; adapt
               no_best+=1
           eta=min(1.0, eta*1.04)
           if no_best>400:
               # perturb accept from best
               a=smooth_perturb(best,rng,0.3)
               c=fast_convolution(a); score=score_from_c(a,c)
               if score>best_score*1.05: a=best.copy(); c=...; score=best_score
               eta=0.25; beta=beta_start*1.5; no_best=0
       else:
           eta=max(0.03, eta*0.8)
           no_best+=1
           if no_best>500:
               a=smooth_perturb(best,rng,0.35)
               c=...; score=...
               if score>best_score*1.08: a=best.copy(); score=best_score
               eta=0.3; beta=beta_start; no_best=0
       # periodic sym try
       if it % 250 == 0:
           sym_a, sym_s=try_sym_variants(a)
           if sym_s < score - 1e-10:
               a=sym_a; c=fast_convolution(a); score=sym_s
               if sym_s < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=sym_s
    # final symtry from best and current
    sym_b,s_b=try_sym_variants(best); if s_b<best_score: best,best_score=sym_b
    sym_c,s_c=try_sym_variants(a); if s_c<best_score: best,best_score=sym_c
    return best,best_score
```
Need top-level `smooth_lowfreq(n, rng, scale)`:
```
def smooth_lowfreq(n, rng, scale):
   if n<4: return zeros
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=zeros
   for m in 1..4:
      amp=scale/(m+1)
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      z += amp*np.cos(2π m t + ph)
   return z
```

def lp_local(a,budget_s,seed=0,max_iters=200):
   n; target; a=normalize; floor; c=conv; score=score_from_c; best=a; best_score=score
   t0; deadline=t0+budget_s
   it=0
   while time<deadline-3 and it<max_iters:
      it+=1
      # recompute c for a? we have but after update yes
      M=float(np.max(c))
      if not finite: break
      a_lp=a.copy(); a_lp[a_lp<floor]=floor; normalize? LP matrix with slight change; use a_lp normalized? It should sum close target. Renormalize to target.
      try:
        C=build_convol(a_lp,n)
        res=linprog(...)
        if res.status !=0: maybe if 1 (timeout) continue/break? We can accept if res.x available.
        g=np.asarray(res.x,float); g=np.clip(g,0,1000)
        V=float(g.sum())
        if V <= target*(1+1e-5): break
        g_target=normalize_sequence(target/V*g,target)
        # evaluate pure g
        g_sc=fast_score_array(g_target)
        update best if g_sc<...
        # t candidates
        t_values=(1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05)
        best_cand=None; best_cand_sc=score
        for t in t_values:
           cand=(1-t)*a + t*g_target
           cand=normalize_sequence(cand,target)
           cand=fast score
           if cand_sc<best_cand_sc:
                best_cand=cand; best_cand_sc=cand_sc
        update best if best_cand_sc<best_score
        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_sc < score*(1-1e-10) or best_cand_sc < best_score*(1-1e-10):
            a=best_cand; c=fast_convolution(a); score=score_from_c(a,c)
        else:
            # no improvement
            break
      except Exception:
         break
   return best,best_score
```

If this fails due data length, `diags` may raise. Wrap build in try; if sparse fails, build dense for n<=1024 else skip LP.
```
def build_conv_matrix(n,a):
   L=2*n-1
   try:
       data=[np.full(n,a[d]) for d in range(n)]
       offsets=(-d for d in range(n)) # generator? diags accepts list
       return diags(data, offsets, shape=(L,n), format='csr')
   except Exception:
       A=np.zeros((L,n)); for d... A[d:d+n,:]=a[d]; return csr_matrix(A)
```
Using generator for offsets maybe okay; use list.

Dense fallback O(n²) memory. If n>1500 and sparse fail, skip.

`softmax_weights`: If beta==0, uniform; if L==0. Use `np.zeros_like(c)`.
```
def softmax_weights(c, beta, maxK=None):
   L=c.size
   if L==0: return np.empty(0)
   m=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(m): return np.ones(L)/L
   if beta <= 0:
      return np.ones(L)/L
   z=beta*(c-m)
   z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
   w=np.exp(z)
   s=float(w.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       if maxK is None:
          maxK=max(1, min(L, int(0.02*L)+1))
       if maxK >= L:
          return np.ones(L)/L
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -maxK)[-maxK:]
       w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0; s=float(maxK)
   return w/s
```
If c has NaN, m nan; return uniform? But sequences finite. Good.

`gradient_weights`:
```
def gradient_weights(a,w):
   n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
   za=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   zw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   g=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(za)*zw,N)[:n]
   np.maximum(g,0.0,out=g)
   return 2.0*g
```
`w` length L. If `w` longer/shorter, `rfft` zero pads/truncates? It uses length N with zeros. Good.

def try_sym_variants(a):
   n=len(a); target=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
   a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
   best=a; sc=fast_score_array(a)
   rev=a[::-1]
   for t in (0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
       if t==0: continue
       cand=normalize_sequence((1.0-t)*a + t*rev,target)
       sc2=fast_score_array(cand)
       if sc2<sc: best=cand; sc=sc2
   return best,sc
```

`smooth_lowfreq`:
```
def smooth_lowfreq(n,rng,scale):
   if n<4: return np.zeros(n)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,5):
      z += (scale/(m+1))*np.cos(2*math.pi*m*t + rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi))
   return z
```

`smooth_perturb`:
```
def smooth_perturb(a,rng,strength):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); n=a.size
   if n>2:
      z=smooth_lowfreq(n,rng,strength*0.6)
      a *= np.exp(z)
   if n>12 and rng.random()<0.35:
      width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//50), max(3,n//5)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width)))
      stop=min(n,start+width)
      a[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, strength, stop-start))
   target=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
   return normalize_sequence(a,target)
```
If width ints with rng.integers high exclusive; ensure low<high. For n small, skip.

def make_binomial(n, reverse=False):
   a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1
   for i in range(1,n): a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
   if reverse: a=a[::-1]
   return normalize_sequence(a, math.sqrt(2*n))
```
If n=1, works? a[0]=1, loop none. Normalize.

`generate_starts`:
```
def generate_starts(n, rng, parent_seq, random_count=40):
   starts=[]
   target=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
   # fallback ones
   add_start_list(starts, np.ones(n), n)
   if parent_seq is not None:
       p=np.asarray(parent_seq,float)
       if p.size>0:
           add_start_list(starts, upsample_sequence(p,n), n)
           add_start_list(starts, upsample_sequence(p,n)[::-1], n)
           up=upsample_sequence(p,n)
           add_start_list(starts, 0.5*(up+up[::-1]), n)
   # binomial and power
   add_start_list(starts, make_binomial(n), n)
   add_start_list(starts, make_binomial(n, True), n)
   for alpha in (0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8):
       add_start_list(starts, make_power(n,alpha), n)
       if alpha in (0.4,0.5,0.6): add reverse
   # geometric random
   for _ in range(6):
       r=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.97)); rev=bool(rng.random()<0.5)
       add_start_list(starts, make_geometric(n,r,rev),n)
   # two block deterministic
   for frac in (0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6): for ratio in (0.4,1.5,3.0): add
   # random harmonics/blocks
   for _ in range(random_count):
       if rng.random()<0.5: add harmonic; else block
   if not starts: add ones
   starts.sort(key=_key_score)
   return starts
```
This may be many (6+15+40=61 plus parents). For n=4096, 70 FFTs ~ okay.

`upsample_sequence`: if parent shape m to n; if m==n return normalize? Use `np.interp`.
```
def upsample_sequence(seq,n):
   m=seq.size
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)
   if m==n: return normalize_sequence(seq, sqrt(2n))
   xold=np.linspace(0,1,m); xnew=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   out=np.interp(xnew,xold,seq)
   return normalize_sequence(out, sqrt(2n))
```
If seq has shape but includes zeros, okay.

`run_level` code:
```
def run_level(n,budget_s,parent,seed=0,pool=None):
   if pool is None: pool=[]
   budget=max(0.0,float(budget_s)); t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
   if budget<20:
       seq = upsample_sequence(parent,n) if parent is not None else np.ones(n)
       sc=fast_score_array(seq)
       add_candidate_pool(pool,seq,sc)
       return seq,sc
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   starts=generate_starts(n,rng,parent, random_count=min(60, max(20, n//32)))
   # quick
   k_quick = max(1, min(4, len(starts), int(budget/120)+1))
   quick_budget = 0.45*budget
   best_seq=None; best_score=inf
   start_quick_time=t0
   for i in range(k_quick):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline-10: break
       elapsed_quick=time.monotonic()-start_quick_time
       remaining_quick=max(1.0, quick_budget-elapsed_quick)
       t_this=remaining_quick/float(k_quick-i)
       sc0, sq0=starts[i]
       try:
           seq,sc=smooth_local(sq0, t_this, seed=seed*100+i, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=300.0, eta0=0.22)
       except Exception:
           seq,sc=sq0,sc0
       if sc<best_score:
           best_seq=seq; best_score=sc
       add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
   if best_seq is None:
       best_seq, best_score = starts[0][1], starts[0][0]
       add_candidate_pool(pool,best_seq,best_score)
   # LP
   rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if n<=2048 and rem>60 and _HAS_LP:
       if n<=1024:
          lp_budget=min(rem*0.45, 350.0)
       else:
          lp_budget=min(rem*0.35, 220.0)
       try:
          seq,sc=lp_local(best_seq, lp_budget, seed=seed+777, max_iters=(200 if n<=640 else 80 if n<=1280 else 30))
          if sc<best_score: best_seq, best_score=seq,sc
          add_candidate_pool(pool, seq,sc)
       except Exception: pass
   # polish
   rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if rem>20 and best_seq is not None:
       try:
          seq,sc=smooth_local(best_seq, rem*0.8, seed=seed+999, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=800.0, eta0=0.12)
          if sc<best_score: best_seq,best_score=seq,sc
          add_candidate_pool(pool,seq,sc)
       except Exception: pass
   # second basin if time
   rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if rem>40 and len(starts)>k_quick:
       try:
           seq,sc=smooth_local(starts[k_quick][1], rem*0.75, seed=seed+1234, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=500.0)
           if sc<best_score: best_seq,best_score=seq,sc
           add_candidate_pool(pool,seq,sc)
       except Exception: pass
   print(...)
   return best_seq,best_score
```

def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):

def sanitize_for_return(seq):
   arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
   if arr.size==0: return [1.0]
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if s < 0.01:
       if s>0: arr = arr * (0.01/s)
       else:
           if arr.size>1: arr = np.ones(arr.size)
           else: arr = np.ones(1)
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   mx=float(arr.max())
   if mx > 1000.0 and mx>0:
       arr *= 1000.0/mx
   # If after scaling sum <0.01? if many zeros, scale up?
   s2=float(arr.sum())
   if s2 < 0.01:
       arr = np.ones_like(arr) if arr.size>0 else np.array([1.0])
   return [float(x) for x in arr]
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); budget=max(10.0,float(budget_s))
    reserve=45.0 if budget>100 else 10.0
    deadline=t0+max(20.0,budget-reserve)
    pool=[]
    parent_arr=None
    try:
       raw = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if raw is not None:
          arr=np.asarray(raw,float)
          if arr.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
             parent_arr=normalize_sequence(np.clip(arr,0,1000), math.sqrt(2.0*arr.size))
             sc=fast_score_array(parent_arr)
             add_candidate_pool(pool,parent_arr,sc)
    except Exception: pass
    fallback_n=512
    if parent_arr is None:
        fb=np.ones(fallback_n); fb=normalize_sequence(fb, sqrt); add
    else:
        add candidate maybe parent
        # also add ones? pool small
    # choose levels
    if budget < 120: levels=[512]
    elif budget < 600: levels=[512,1024]
    elif budget < 1500: levels=[512,1024,2048]
    else: levels=[512,1024,2048,4096]
    weights = [0.7,1.0,1.7,4.0]
    # if budget shorter, use first len weights normalized? maybe scale = available / sum(weights[:len])
    avail=deadline-time
    if pool: global_best=pool[0][1]
    else global_best=fallback
    weights_sum=sum(weights[:len(levels)])
    for i,n in enumerate(levels):
       b=avail*weights[i]/weights_sum
       if b<25: break
       time_before=time.monotonic()
       parent = pool[0][1] if pool else global_best
       try: seq,sc=run_level(n,b,parent,seed=1000+i*17,pool=pool)
       except Exception: continue
       if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < pool[0][1][0]? pool sorted; no need.
       # if time remaining <= reserve break
    # final exact
    final_seq = sanitize_for_return(pool[0][1] if pool else parent)
    final_score=inf
    candidates = pool[:6]
    # include parent and fallback maybe already
    for sc, arr in candidates:
       arr_ll=sanitize_for_return(arr)
       try: ex=float(evaluate_sequence(arr_ll))

    # If final_score inf, fallback ones or parent list

Double-check `make_block_sequence`: If `edges=np.unique(edges)` can reduce num_blocks; `vals=rng.uniform(0.05,2.0,num_blocks)`. If num_blocks==0 (n=1?) return normalize ones. For n<4, return ones.
```
def make_block_sequence(n, rng, num_blocks=None):
   if n<4: return normalize(np.ones(n), sqrt)
   if num_blocks is None: num_blocks=int(rng.integers(4, min(20,n//2)+1))
   num_blocks=max(2,min(num_blocks,n//2))
   edges=np.sort(rng.integers(0,n+1,num_blocks+1))
   edges[0]=0; edges[-1]=n
   edges=np.unique(edges)
   if edges[-1]!=n: edges=np.append(edges,n); edges=np.unique(edges)
   lengths=np.diff(edges)
   nb=len(lengths)
   if nb==0: return normalize(np.ones(n), sqrt)
   vals=rng.uniform(0.05,2.0,nb)
   if rng.random()<0.2: vals[int(rng.integers(0,nb))]=0.0
   a=np.repeat(vals,lengths)
   return normalize_sequence(a,sqrt(2n))
```
If unique removes endpoints? we add.

`make_harmonic`: if n<2 return ones.
```
z = rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)
for m in 1..max_freq:
   amp = rng.uniform(0, 1.2)/(m**0.7)
   ph = rng.uniform(0,2pi)
   z += amp*np.cos(2π m t + ph)
# add an anti-symmetric term to help
z += rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.sin(π t)
z += rng.uniform(-1,1)*(2t-1)
z -= np.max(z)
a=np.exp(z)
```
This may create strong boundary. Good.

`make_geometric`: if r<=0? r uniform. For r very close1, a ~ ones; normalize. If reverse, a[::-1].

`make_two_block`: If m <1 or >=n-1 adjust. Denom: m*ratio + (n-m). But if ratio? A=m? We want sum target. Denom = m*ratio + (n-m); B=target/denom; A=ratio*B. Fill. Good.

Now, exact candidate pool. In `add_candidate_pool`, if arrays not normalized? We should not modify. It copies.

Potential issue: The function `normalize_sequence` modifies input copy. In starts, we pass arrays maybe not target; okay.

Now, possible problem with `time.monotonic` inside loops and global budget. Good.

Let's now formulate final code.

After final, ensure code block only. No analysis.

Let's include docstring at top summarizing.

One more thought: The objective `evaluate_sequence` uses `max(b_sequence)` over full convolution. Our `fast_score_array` uses same but if sequence values below 0.01 sum? returns inf. Good.

Performance of exact evaluation on 4096 arrays: if in final loop we call `evaluate_sequence` on `sanitize` list, each is Python list of 4096 floats. It loops Python over list checking types, may be 4096*5=20k operations, okay. `np.convolve` maybe. For 4 candidates ~ ok.

Now, potential hidden: The environment may run `propose_candidate()` with `budget_s` parameter but as keyword? We define. Good.

Let's write code.

Double-check no `lambda`. Good.

Let's think if using `np.float64`? okay.

Potential issue in `smooth_local`: `beta_end/beta_start` if beta_start 0? We set >0.

`smooth_local` uses `prog = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(1e-6,budget_s))`; if budget_s tiny. Good.

`smooth_local` `no_best` increments; if no_best > 500, reset. But if budget short, may never.

`try_sym_variants` calls `fast_score_array` which recomputes conv. In `smooth_local` initial, it may improve before main loop. Good.

Now, exact final: If `evaluate_sequence` of a candidate returns less but candidate values clipped? We sanitize. If two exact ties, keep first.

Let's produce final code.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    F(a) = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2

using a hybrid of:
  - deterministic analytic starts (binomial / power-law / harmonic / block),
  - FFT-based smooth local search with annealed log-sum-exp subgradients,
  - a linear-programming best-response iteration inspired by the stated SOTA,
  - coarse-to-fine sequence lengths and exact validation near the deadline.

The public entry point is propose_candidate().  It returns a Python list of
finite, clamped, non-negative floats.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    _HAS_LP = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_LP = False

_LP_STRUCT_CACHE = {}


def _key_score(item):
    return item[0]


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sequence(a, target_sum, cap=1000.0):
    """Clip to [0, cap], renormalize to target_sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, cap)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a.fill(float(target_sum) / float(a.size))
        return a

    for _ in range(6):
        a *= float(target_sum) / s
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, cap)
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if abs(s2 - float(target_sum)) <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(float(target_sum))):
            return a
        s = s2

    if s > 0.0:
        a *= float(target_sum) / s
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, cap)
    return a


def fast_convolution(a):
    """FFT-based linear convolution a * a, length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def score_from_c(a, c):
    """Evaluation analogue from an already computed convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")

    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def fast_score_array(a):
    """Fast analogue of evaluate_sequence() for numpy arrays."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def cross_corr_weights(a, w):
    """
    Return gradient 2*g of the weighted log-sum-exp surrogate, where
    g_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}.  Uses FFT cross-correlation.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(max(L, n))
    za = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    zw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    g = np.fft.irfft(np.conjugate(za) * zw, N)[:n]
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return 2.0 * g


def softmax_weights(c, beta, max_k=None):
    """Normalized softmax over convolution coefficients, with top-k fallback."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    z = beta * (c - m)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        if max_k is None:
            max_k = max(1, min(L, int(0.02 * L) + 1))
        if max_k >= L:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -max_k)[-max_k:]
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        s = float(max_k)

    return w / s


def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Linspace-resample to length n and normalize to sqrt(2n)."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    m = seq.size
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))

    if m == 0:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), target)
    if m == n:
        return normalize_sequence(seq, target)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(xnew, xold, seq)
    return normalize_sequence(out, target)


def smooth_lowfreq(n, rng, scale):
    """Low-frequency smooth random log-domain noise."""
    if n < 4 or scale <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 5):
        amp = scale / float(m + 1)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)
    return z


def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    """Multiplicative smooth perturbation of a sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))

    if n > 2:
        a *= np.exp(smooth_lowfreq(n, rng, strength * 0.55))

    if n > 12 and rng.random() < 0.35:
        low_w = max(2, n // 80)
        high_w = max(low_w + 1, n // 8 + 1)
        if high_w > n:
            high_w = n
        width = int(rng.integers(low_w, min(high_w, n)))
        low_s = 0
        high_s = max(1, n - width + 1)
        start = int(rng.integers(low_s, high_s))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        a[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, stop - start))

    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def try_sym_variants(a):
    """Test reversal-mixed variants; return the best among them."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    best = a
    best_score = fast_score_array(a)

    rev = a[::-1]
    for t in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
        cand = normalize_sequence((1.0 - t) * a + t * rev, target)
        sc = fast_score_array(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc

    return best, best_score


def make_binomial(n, reverse=False):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated.  This gives a strong baseline
    whose square is flat on the first half of the support.
    """
    n = int(n)
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * float(2 * i - 1) / float(2 * i)
    if reverse:
        a = a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def make_power(n, alpha, reverse=False):
    """Power-law decaying start."""
    n = int(n)
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = x ** (-float(alpha))
    if reverse:
        a = a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def make_geometric(n, r, reverse=False):
    """Geometric start."""
    n = int(n)
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    r = max(0.01, min(0.999, float(r)))
    a = r ** i
    if reverse:
        a = a[::-1].copy()
    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def make_two_block(n, frac, ratio):
    """Simple two-level block start."""
    n = int(n)
    m = int(round(frac * n))
    m = max(1, min(n - 1, m))
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))

    denom = m * float(ratio) + float(n - m)
    if denom <= 0.0:
        b = 1.0
    else:
        b = target / denom
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = b * float(ratio)
    a[m:] = b
    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def make_harmonic(n, rng, max_freq=6):
    """Random low-frequency positive curve via exp of smooth noise."""
    n = int(n)
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), target)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for m in range(1, max_freq + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.2) / float((m + 1) ** 0.7)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)

    z += float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * np.sin(math.pi * t)
    z += float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def make_block(n, rng):
    """Random piecewise-constant start."""
    n = int(n)
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    if n < 4:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), target)

    nb = int(rng.integers(4, max(5, min(20, n // 2) + 1)))
    nb = max(2, min(nb, n // 2))

    edges = np.sort(rng.integers(0, n + 1, nb + 1))
    edges[0] = 0
    edges[-1] = n
    edges = np.unique(edges)
    if edges[-1] != n:
        edges = np.append(edges, n)
        edges = np.unique(edges)

    lengths = np.diff(edges)
    nb = int(lengths.size)
    if nb <= 0:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), target)

    vals = rng.uniform(0.05, 2.0, nb)
    if rng.random() < 0.20:
        vals[int(rng.integers(0, nb))] = 0.0

    a = np.repeat(vals, lengths)
    return normalize_sequence(a, target)


def add_start_list(starts, a, n):
    """Evaluate and append a candidate if finite."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * int(n)))
        arr = normalize_sequence(arr, target)
        sc = fast_score_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((float(sc), arr))
    except Exception:
        pass


def generate_starts(n, rng, parent_seq, random_count=50):
    """Create a diverse pool of starts for length n."""
    n = int(n)
    starts = []

    add_start_list(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    if parent_seq is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent_seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.size > 0:
            up = upsample_sequence(p, n)
            add_start_list(starts, up, n)
            add_start_list(starts, up[::-1], n)
            add_start_list(starts, 0.5 * (up + up[::-1]), n)

    add_start_list(starts, make_binomial(n), n)
    add_start_list(starts, make_binomial(n, True), n)

    for alpha in (0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80):
        add_start_list(starts, make_power(n, alpha), n)
        if alpha in (0.40, 0.50, 0.60):
            add_start_list(starts, make_power(n, alpha, True), n)

    for _ in range(6):
        r = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.98))
        rev = bool(rng.random() < 0.5)
        add_start_list(starts, make_geometric(n, r, rev), n)

    for frac in (0.25, 0.40, 0.50, 0.62):
        for ratio in (0.40, 1.50, 3.00):
            add_start_list(starts, make_two_block(n, frac, ratio), n)

    for _ in range(max(0, int(random_count))):
        if rng.random() < 0.52:
            add_start_list(starts, make_harmonic(n, rng, max_freq=6), n)
        else:
            add_start_list(starts, make_block(n, rng), n)

    if not starts:
        starts.append((2.0, normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), math.sqrt(2.0 * n))))

    starts.sort(key=_key_score)
    return starts


def _get_lp_structure(n):
    """Return fixed row/col index arrays for the convolution LP matrix."""
    n = int(n)
    if n not in _LP_STRUCT_CACHE:
        cols = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
        rows = cols + np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
        _LP_STRUCT_CACHE[n] = (rows, cols)
        if len(_LP_STRUCT_CACHE) > 6:
            for k in list(_LP_STRUCT_CACHE.keys())[:1]:
                del _LP_STRUCT_CACHE[k]
    return _LP_STRUCT_CACHE[n]


def build_conv_lp_matrix(n, a):
    """
    Build the n x (2n-1) lower-banded matrix C with C[k,i] = a[k-i]
    when valid, and 0 otherwise.
    """
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    rows, cols = _get_lp_structure(n)
    data = np.repeat(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), n)
    return csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(L, n))


def solve_lp_unit(a, n, time_limit=10.0):
    """
    Solve max sum(g) subject to (a*g) <= 1, g >= 0.
    Returns (g, value, res) or (None, None, None) on failure.
    """
    if not _HAS_LP:
        return None, None, None

    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        C = build_conv_lp_matrix(n, a)
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)

        opts = {
            "presolve": True,
            "time_limit": float(max(1.0, min(15.0, time_limit))),
            "output_flag": 0,
        }
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=C,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=(0.0, None),
            method="highs",
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            C = build_conv_lp_matrix(n, a)
            c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            b_ub = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=C,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=(0.0, None),
                method="highs",
                options={"output_flag": 0},
            )
        except Exception:
            return None, None, None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None, None, None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)

    val = float(g.sum())
    if val <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(val):
        return None, None, res

    return g, val, res


def lp_local(a, budget_s, seed=0, max_iters=120):
    """
    LP best-response iteration:
      solve max sum(g) s.t. a*g <= 1,
      normalize g to the same total mass as a,
      move from a toward g by line search.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)
    floor = max(1e-10, target * 1e-8)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    best = a.copy()
    best_c = fast_convolution(best)
    best_score = score_from_c(best, best_c)

    current = best.copy()
    current_c = best_c.copy()
    current_score = best_score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0 and it < int(max_iters):
        it += 1
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            current = best.copy()
            current_c = best_c.copy()
            current_score = best_score

        a_lp = np.maximum(current, floor)
        a_lp = normalize_sequence(a_lp, target)

        g, val, _ = solve_lp_unit(a_lp, n, time_limit=max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
        if g is None:
            break

        g_target = normalize_sequence((float(target) / val) * g, target)
        g_c = fast_convolution(g_target)
        g_score = score_from_c(g_target, g_c)

        improved = False
        if np.isfinite(g_score) and g_score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
            best = g_target.copy()
            best_c = g_c.copy()
            best_score = g_score
            improved = True

        # Line search among mixes of current a and LP response g.
        t_values = (1.0, 0.85, 0.65, 0.45, 0.25, 0.10, 0.03)
        local_best_cand = current
        local_best_score = current_score
        for tt in t_values:
            cand = (1.0 - tt) * current + tt * g_target
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cand = normalize_sequence(cand, target)
            cc = fast_convolution(cand)
            sc = score_from_c(cand, cc)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                local_best_cand = cand
                local_best_score = sc

        if np.isfinite(local_best_score) and local_best_score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
            best = local_best_cand.copy()
            best_c = fast_convolution(best)
            best_score = local_best_score
            improved = True

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(local_best_score) and local_best_score < current_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
            current = local_best_cand.copy()
            current_c = fast_convolution(current)
            current_score = local_best_score
            accept = True

        if not accept and current_score > best_score * (1.0 + 1e-10):
            current = best.copy()
            current_c = best_c.copy()
            current_score = best_score

        if not accept and not improved:
            break

        if it % 10 == 0:
            print(f"[lp_local] n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.7f} current={current_score:.7f}")

    # Final symmetrization test.
    sym_a, sym_s = try_sym_variants(best)
    if np.isfinite(sym_s) and sym_s < best_score:
        best = sym_a
        best_score = sym_s

    return best, float(best_score)


def smooth_local(a, budget_s, seed=0, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=300.0, eta0=0.22):
    """
    Multiplicative / log-domain local search minimizing the exact max score,
    using annealed log-sum-exp smoothed subgradients.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    target = math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * n))
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)
    floor = max(1e-14, target * 1e-12)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target)

    current = a.copy()
    current_c = fast_convolution(current)
    current_score = score_from_c(current, current_c)

    best = current.copy()
    best_c = current_c.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    # Initial symmetrization.
    sym_a, sym_s = try_sym_variants(current)
    if np.isfinite(sym_s) and sym_s < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
        best = sym_a.copy()
        best_c = fast_convolution(best)
        best_score = sym_s
        current = best.copy()
        current_c = best_c.copy()
        current_score = best_score

    eta = float(eta0)
    beta = float(beta_start)
    no_best = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.4:
        it += 1
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            current = best.copy()
            current_c = fast_convolution(current)
            current_score = score_from_c(current, current_c)

        if budget_s > 1.0:
            prog = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(1e-9, float(budget_s)))
            beta = float(beta_start) * (float(beta_end) / float(beta_start)) ** prog

        weights = softmax_weights(current_c, beta)
        grad = cross_corr_weights(current, weights)
        lam = float(np.dot(current, grad)) / target
        d = grad - lam
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))

        if scale <= 1e-14:
            no_best += 1
            if no_best > 80:
                current = smooth_perturb(best, rng, 0.30)
                current = np.maximum(current, floor)
                current = normalize_sequence(current, target)
                current_c = fast_convolution(current)
                current_score = score_from_c(current, current_c)
                if current_score > best_score * 1.05:
                    current = best.copy()
                    current_c = best_c.copy()
                    current_score = best_score
                eta = max(eta, 0.22)
                no_best = 0
            continue

        step = (-eta / scale) * d

        noise_amp = 0.025 * math.exp(-0.45 * min(1.0, it / 2500.0))
        if noise_amp > 1e-5:
            step += noise_amp * smooth_lowfreq(n, rng, 0.30)

        step = np.clip(step, -2.5, 2.5)
        cand = current * np.exp(step)
        cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
        cand = normalize_sequence(cand, target)
        cand_c = fast_convolution(cand)
        cand_score = score_from_c(cand, cand_c)

        if np.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score <= current_score * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12:
            current = cand
            current_c = cand_c
            current_score = cand_score

            if cand_score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                best = current.copy()
                best_c = current_c.copy()
                best_score = cand_score
                no_best = 0
            else:
                no_best += 1

            eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.045)
        else:
            eta = max(0.025, eta * 0.82)
            no_best += 1

        if no_best > 500:
            current = smooth_perturb(best, rng, 0.35)
            current = np.maximum(current, floor)
            current = normalize_sequence(current, target)
            current_c = fast_convolution(current)
            current_score = score_from_c(current, current_c)
            if current_score > best_score * 1.08:
                current = best.copy()
                current_c = best_c.copy()
                current_score = best_score
            eta = 0.25
            beta = max(float(beta_start), float(beta) * 0.5)
            no_best = 0

        if it % 350 == 0:
            sym_a, sym_s = try_sym_variants(current)
            if np.isfinite(sym_s) and sym_s < current_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                current = sym_a.copy()
                current_c = fast_convolution(current)
                current_score = score_from_c(current, current_c)
                if current_score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                    best = current.copy()
                    best_c = current_c.copy()
                    best_score = current_score

    # Final transformation tests from best and current basins.
    sym_a, sym_s = try_sym_variants(best)
    if np.isfinite(sym_s) and sym_s < best_score:
        best = sym_a
        best_score = sym_s

    sym_a, sym_s = try_sym_variants(current)
    if np.isfinite(sym_s) and sym_s < best_score:
        best = sym_a
        best_score = sym_s

    c_best = fast_convolution(best)
    best_score = score_from_c(best, c_best)
    return best, float(best_score)


def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):
    """Maintain a small sorted pool of promising sequences."""
    if pool is None or seq is None or score is None:
        return
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return

    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return

    pool.append((sc, arr))
    pool.sort(key=_key_score)
    if len(pool) > 18:
        pool.pop()


def run_level(n, budget_s, parent_seq, seed=0, pool=None):
    """Run a coarse-to-fine optimization stage at a given length."""
    n = int(n)
    if pool is None:
        pool = []

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget

    if budget < 20.0:
        seq = upsample_sequence(parent_seq, n) if parent_seq is not None else normalize_sequence(np.ones(n), math.sqrt(2.0 * n))
        sc = fast_score_array(seq)
        add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
        return seq, sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    random_count = int(min(70, max(24, n // 24)))
    starts = generate_starts(n, rng, parent_seq, random_count=random_count)

    k_quick = max(1, min(4, len(starts), int(budget / 120.0) + 1))
    quick_budget = 0.45 * budget
    quick_start_t = time.monotonic()

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for i in range(k_quick):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 12.0:
            break

        remaining_quick = max(1.0, quick_budget - (time.monotonic() - quick_start_t))
        time_this = remaining_quick / float(k_quick - i)
        time_this = max(5.0, time_this)
        time_this = min(time_this, deadline - time.monotonic() - 6.0)

        sc0, sq0 = starts[i]
        try:
            seq, sc = smooth_local(
                sq0,
                time_this,
                seed=int(seed * 1000 + 11 + i),
                beta_start=8.0,
                beta_end=300.0,
                eta0=0.22,
            )
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = sq0, sc0

        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)

        add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = starts[0][1].copy()
        best_score = starts[0][0]
        add_candidate_pool(pool, best_seq, best_score)

    # LP polishing at moderate lengths.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if _HAS_LP and n <= 2048 and rem > 70.0:
        if n <= 1024:
            lp_budget = min(rem * 0.48, 420.0)
            lp_iters = 500
        else:
            lp_budget = min(rem * 0.36, 240.0)
            lp_iters = 120

        try:
            seq, sc = lp_local(best_seq, lp_budget, seed=int(seed + 555), max_iters=lp_iters)
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
            add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
            print(f"[level {n}] after lp best={best_score:.7f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final smooth polish of the best basin found at this level.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 20.0:
        try:
            seq, sc = smooth_local(
                best_seq,
                rem * 0.82,
                seed=int(seed + 999),
                beta_start=30.0,
                beta_end=800.0,
                eta0=0.12,
            )
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
            add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Optional second basin if time remains.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 50.0 and len(starts) > k_quick:
        idx = min(len(starts) - 1, k_quick + max(1, len(starts) // 10))
        try:
            seq, sc = smooth_local(
                starts[idx][1],
                rem * 0.78,
                seed=int(seed + 1234),
                beta_start=12.0,
                beta_end=500.0,
                eta0=0.18,
            )
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                best_score = float(sc)
            add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"[level {n}] budget={budget:.1f} best={best_score:.7f}")
    return best_seq, float(best_score)


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """
    Return a valid Python list of non-negative finite floats.
    Do not forcibly rescale to mean 1; keep scale moderate to avoid 1000 clipping.
    """
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return [1.0]

    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        if s > 0.0:
            seq *= 0.01 / s
        else:
            seq = np.ones(max(1, int(seq.size)), dtype=np.float64)
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    mx = float(seq.max())
    if mx > 1000.0:
        if mx > 0.0:
            seq *= 1000.0 / mx
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s2 = float(seq.sum())
    if s2 < 0.01:
        seq = np.ones(max(1, int(seq.size)), dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in seq]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Runs coarse-to-fine search for up to budget_s seconds
    and returns the best sequence found.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))

    # Reserve time for exact evaluation and Python list conversion.
    reserve = 60.0 if budget > 300.0 else 20.0
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - reserve)

    pool = []

    # Seed with the provided construction if available.
    parent_seq = None
    try:
        raw_parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw_parent is not None:
            p = np.asarray(raw_parent, dtype=np.float64)
            if p.size > 0 and np.any(np.isfinite(p)):
                p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
                p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if p.sum() > 0.0:
                    p = normalize_sequence(p, math.sqrt(max(1.0, 2.0 * p.size)))
                    pc = fast_convolution(p)
                    ps = score_from_c(p, pc)
                    add_candidate_pool(pool, p, ps)
                    parent_seq = p
                    print(f"[init] parent fast score={ps:.7f} n={p.size}")
    except Exception:
        parent_seq = None

    # Always have a trivial fallback.
    fallback_n = 512
    fallback = normalize_sequence(np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64), math.sqrt(2.0 * fallback_n))
    fs = fast_score_array(fallback)
    add_candidate_pool(pool, fallback, fs)

    if parent_seq is not None:
        global_best = parent_seq.copy()
    elif pool:
        global_best = pool[0][1].copy()
    else:
        global_best = fallback.copy()

    # Choose coarse-to-fine lengths.
    budget_now = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if budget_now < 160.0:
        levels = [512]
    elif budget_now < 700.0:
        levels = [512, 1024]
    elif budget_now < 1600.0:
        levels = [512, 1024, 2048]
    else:
        levels = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096]

    if len(levels) == 1:
        weights = [1.0]
    elif len(levels) == 2:
        weights = [1.1, 1.7]
    elif len(levels) == 3:
        weights = [1.4, 1.2, 2.5]
    else:
        weights = [1.5, 1.2, 2.0, 1.8]

    total_weight = float(sum(weights))
    available = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    for idx, level_n in enumerate(levels):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
            break

        b = available * float(weights[idx]) / total_weight
        if b < 30.0:
            b = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
            if b < 15.0:
                break

        # Parent for this level is the best known candidate so far.
        level_parent = pool[0][1] if pool else global_best

        try:
            seq, sc = run_level(
                int(level_n),
                b,
                level_parent,
                seed=int(917 + idx * 1237),
                pool=pool,
            )
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = None, float("inf")

        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            if pool:
                global_best = pool[0][1].copy()
            else:
                global_best = seq.copy()

    # If time remains, give the global best one more polish at its own length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 45.0 and pool:
        best_seq = pool[0][1].copy()
        try:
            seq, sc = smooth_local(
                best_seq,
                rem * 0.9,
                seed=int(seed + 8080),
                beta_start=40.0,
                beta_end=900.0,
                eta0=0.10,
            )
            add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    final_seq = sanitize_for_return(pool[0][1] if pool else global_best)
    final_score = float("inf")

    # Exact validation of a shortlist.  Prefer interpolating the real evaluator.
    shortlist = pool[:6]
    # Make sure the original parent is considered if it exists.
    if parent_seq is not None:
        shorts = []
        seen = False
        for item in shortlist:
            if np.allclose(item[1], parent_seq, rtol=0.0, atol=0.0):
                seen = True
            shorts.append(item)
        if not seen:
            shorts.append((fast_score_array(parent_seq), parent_seq.copy()))
        shortlist = shorts[:7]

    for _, arr in shortlist:
        try:
            lst = sanitize_for_return(arr)
        except Exception:
            lst = final_seq

        try:
            ex = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            ex = float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < final_score:
            final_score = ex
            final_seq = lst

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        # Last-resort valid list.
        if parent_seq is not None:
            final_seq = sanitize_for_return(parent_seq)
        else:
            final_seq = sanitize_for_return(fallback)

    # If exact was available, print achieved bound.
    try:
        if np.isfinite(final_score):
            print(f"[final] exact score={final_score:.7f} n={len(final_seq)}")
        else:
            # No exact bound computed; return best fast-score sequence.
            print(f"[final] no exact finite value, returning best fast candidate")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return final_seq
```
